    getgenv().Eclipse_ApplySounds = function()
        local ns = getgenv().FTF_Sounds
        if not ns then return end
        FootstepVolume = ns.FootstepVolume
        JumpVolume = ns.JumpVolume
        FallVolume = ns.FallVolume
        MuteFootsteps = ns.MuteFootsteps
        MuteJump = ns.MuteJump
        MuteFall = ns.MuteFall
        MuteHit = ns.MuteHit
        FootstepMinDist = ns.FootstepMinDist
        FootstepMaxDist = ns.FootstepMaxDist
        FootstepRollOff = ns.FootstepRollOff
        SelectedSounds = { Footsteps = ns.SelectedFootsteps, Jump = ns.SelectedJump, Fall = ns.SelectedFall }
        if sliders["FootSteps"] then sliders["FootSteps"].SetValue(FootstepVolume) end
        if sliders["Jump"] then sliders["Jump"].SetValue(JumpVolume) end
        if sliders["Fall"] then sliders["Fall"].SetValue(FallVolume) end
        if sliders["Min Distância"] then sliders["Min Distância"].SetValue(FootstepMinDist) end
        if sliders["Max Distância"] then sliders["Max Distância"].SetValue(FootstepMaxDist) end
        if sliders["RollOff"] then sliders["RollOff"].SetValue(FootstepRollOff) end
        if toggleSetters["Mute Your Footsteps"] then toggleSetters["Mute Your Footsteps"](MuteFootsteps) end
        if toggleSetters["Mute Your Jump"] then toggleSetters["Mute Your Jump"](MuteJump) end
        if toggleSetters["Mute Your Fall"] then toggleSetters["Mute Your Fall"](MuteFall) end
        if toggleSetters["Mute Hit Sound"] then toggleSetters["Mute Hit Sound"](MuteHit) end
        for _, t in pairs({"Footsteps", "Jump", "Fall"}) do
            for _, b in ipairs(ActiveButtons[t]) do b.UpdateVisual() end
        end
        if musicSound then musicSound.Volume = ns.MusicVolume or 0.5 end
        if musicVolFill then musicVolFill.Size = UDim2.new(ns.MusicVolume or 0.5, 0, 1, 0) end
        if musicVolPercent then musicVolPercent.Text = math.floor((ns.MusicVolume or 0.5) * 100).. "%" end
        if musicIdBox then musicIdBox.Text = ns.MusicId or "" end
        ApplySoundIdGlobally("Footsteps", SelectedSounds.Footsteps)
        ApplySoundIdGlobally("Jump", SelectedSounds.Jump)
        ApplySoundIdGlobally("Fall", SelectedSounds.Fall)
        UpdateAllSounds()
    end

    task.wait(1)
    ApplySoundIdGlobally("Footsteps", SelectedSounds.Footsteps)
    ApplySoundIdGlobally("Jump", SelectedSounds.Jump)
    ApplySoundIdGlobally("Fall", SelectedSounds.Fall)
    UpdateAllSounds()
end)

    local notifyThread = nil
    local function Notify(msg)
        if not gui then return end
        local old = gui:FindFirstChild("SkinNotify")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        if notifyThread then pcall(function() task.cancel(notifyThread) end) notifyThread = nil end
        local holder = Instance.new("Frame", gui)
        holder.Name = "SkinNotify"
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 0, 38)
        holder.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -140, 0, -50)
        holder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10)
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
        holder.ZIndex = 2000
        holder.ClipsDescendants = true
        Instance.new("UICorner", holder).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", holder)
        s.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
        s.Thickness = 1
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", holder)
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = "" .. msg
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lbl.TextSize = 13
        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        lbl.ZIndex = 2001
        TweenService:Create(holder, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -140, 0, 20)}):Play()
        notifyThread = task.delay(2, function()
            notifyThread = nil
            if holder and holder.Parent then
                local t = TweenService:Create(holder, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -140, 0, -50)})
                t:Play()
                t.Completed:Connect(function() if holder then holder:Destroy() end end)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function CreateTopInput(parent, placeholder)
        local holder = Instance.new("Frame", parent)

        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.45}):Play() end)

    local function CreateTopToggle(parent, text, imageUrl, cb)
        local btn = CreateTopCard(parent, text, imageUrl, function() end)
        local on = false
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() on = not on cb(on) end)
        return btn, function(v) on = v end
    end

    local OriginalRightLegMesh = nil
    local CurrentLegBundle = nil
    local SavedHeadMesh = nil
    local SavedHeadFace = nil
    local SavedHeadTransparency = 0
    local HeadlessLoop = nil
    local HeadlessOn = false
    local RoyalOn = false
    local SetRoyalToggle = function() end
    local SetHeadlessToggle = function() end
    local SetLegTogglesReset = function() end
    local CacheAssets = {}
    local function GetAsset(id)
        if CacheAssets[id] then return CacheAssets[id]:Clone() end
        local ok, obj = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://"..tostring(id))[1] end)
        if ok and obj then CacheAssets[id] = obj return obj:Clone() end
        return nil
    end
    local function SaveOriginalLeg(char)
        if OriginalRightLegMesh then return end
        for _, v in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("CharacterMesh") and v.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.RightLeg then OriginalRightLegMesh = v:Clone() break end
        end
    end
    local function RemoveCurrentLeg(char)
        for _, v in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("CharacterMesh") and v.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.RightLeg then v:Destroy() end
        end
    end
    local function RestoreLeg(char)
        RemoveCurrentLeg(char)
        if OriginalRightLegMesh then OriginalRightLegMesh:Clone().Parent = char end
        CurrentLegBundle = nil
    end

    local function ClearAccessoryStates()
        local char = Player.Character
        if HeadlessLoop then HeadlessLoop:Disconnect() HeadlessLoop = nil end
        HeadlessOn = false
        SetHeadlessToggle(false)
        if char then
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head then head.Transparency = 0 head.CanCollide = true end
        end
        SavedHeadMesh = nil
        SavedHeadFace = nil
        if char then
            for _, v in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if (v:IsA("Accessory") or v:IsA("Hat") or v:IsA("Tool")) and (v.Name == "RoyalScepter" or v.Name == "Royal Scepter") then v:Destroy() end
            end
        end
        RoyalOn = false
        SetRoyalToggle(false)
        OriginalRightLegMesh = nil
        CurrentLegBundle = nil
        SetLegTogglesReset()
        if getgenv().SkinFixLoop then getgenv().SkinFixLoop:Disconnect() getgenv().SkinFixLoop = nil end
    end

    local legState = { Korblox = false, SkeletonLeg = false, ZombieLeg = false }
    local legSetters = {}
    local function SetLegToggle(key, bundleId, legName, on)
        legState[key] = on
        if on then
            for k,_ in pairs(legState) do if k ~= key then legState[k] = false end end
            ApplyLegOnlyFunc(bundleId, legName)
        else
            local char = Player.Character
            if char then RestoreLeg(char) end
            Notify("Removido: " .. legName)
        end
    end
    SetLegTogglesReset = function()
        legState.Korblox = false legState.SkeletonLeg = false legState.ZombieLeg = false
        for _, s in pairs(legSetters) do s(false) end
    end

    if getgenv().SkinFixLoop then getgenv().SkinFixLoop:Disconnect() getgenv().SkinFixLoop = nil end

    local function SmartWeld(char, accessory)
        local handle = accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if not handle then return end
        handle.Anchored = false
        handle.CanCollide = false
        handle.Massless = true
        accessory.Parent = char
        local accAtt = handle:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Attachment")
        if not accAtt then
            local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
            weld.Part0 = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            weld.Part1 = handle
            weld.C0 = CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0)
            weld.Parent = handle
            return
        end
        local targetPart = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local charAtt = targetPart and targetPart:FindFirstChild(accAtt.Name)
        if not charAtt then
            targetPart = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            charAtt = targetPart and targetPart:FindFirstChild(accAtt.Name)
        end
        local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
        weld.Part1 = handle
        weld.Part0 = targetPart or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        weld.C0 = charAtt and charAtt.CFrame or CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0)
        weld.C1 = accAtt.CFrame
        weld.Parent = handle
    end
    local function WeldBack(char, accessory)
        local handle = accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if not handle then return end
        handle.Anchored = false
        handle.CanCollide = false
        handle.Massless = true
        accessory.Parent = char
        local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if not torso then return end
        local w = Instance.new("Weld")
        w.Part0 = torso
        w.Part1 = handle
        w.C0 = CFrame.new(0.25, -0.1, 0.6)
        w.C1 = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
        w.Parent = handle
    end
    local function StartFixLoop(char, colorTable, originalHeadTextureId)
        if getgenv().SkinFixLoop then getgenv().SkinFixLoop:Disconnect() end
        local lastCheck = 0
        getgenv().SkinFixLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if tick() - lastCheck < 0.5 then return end
            lastCheck = tick()
            if not char or not char.Parent then
                if getgenv().SkinFixLoop then getgenv().SkinFixLoop:Disconnect() end
                return
            end
            if HeadlessOn then return end
            for partName, color in pairs(colorTable) do
                local part = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
                if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Color ~= color then
                    part.Color = color
                    part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                    if partName == "Head" then
                        local mesh = part:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
                        if mesh then
                            if originalHeadTextureId ~= "" then
                                if mesh.TextureId ~= originalHeadTextureId then mesh.TextureId = originalHeadTextureId end
                            elseif mesh.TextureId ~= "" then mesh.TextureId = "" end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    local function ApplyDesc(desc)
        local char = Player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local dummy = Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(desc, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6)
        dummy.Name = "AssetSource"
        dummy.Parent = nil
        task.wait(0.3)
        local realColors = { ["Head"] = desc.HeadColor, ["Torso"] = desc.TorsoColor, ["Left Arm"] = desc.LeftArmColor, ["Right Arm"] = desc.RightArmColor, ["Left Leg"] = desc.LeftLegColor, ["Right Leg"] = desc.RightLegColor }
        local targetHeadTexture = ""
        local dummyMesh = dummy.Head:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
        if dummyMesh then targetHeadTexture = dummyMesh.TextureId end
        if char:FindFirstChild("Head") then char.Head.Transparency = 0 char.Head.CanCollide = true end
        for _, v in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("Accessory") or v:IsA("Hat") or v:IsA("Shirt") or v:IsA("Pants") or v:IsA("ShirtGraphic") or v:IsA("CharacterMesh") or v:IsA("BodyColors") then v:Destroy() end
        end
        if char:FindFirstChild("Head") and char.Head:FindFirstChild("face") then char.Head.face:Destroy() end
        local myMesh = char.Head:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh") or Instance.new("SpecialMesh", char.Head)
        if dummyMesh then
            myMesh.MeshType = dummyMesh.MeshType
            myMesh.MeshId = dummyMesh.MeshId
            myMesh.Scale = dummyMesh.Scale
            myMesh.TextureId = targetHeadTexture
        else
            myMesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Head
            myMesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1.25, 1.25, 1.25)
            myMesh.TextureId = ""
        end
        myMesh.VertexColor = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
        for _, item in pairs(dummy:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("CharacterMesh") or item:IsA("Shirt") or item:IsA("Pants") or item:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then item:Clone().Parent = char end
        end
        local faceDecal = Instance.new("Decal")
        faceDecal.Name = "face"
        local dummyFace = dummy.Head:FindFirstChild("face")
        faceDecal.Texture = dummyFace and dummyFace.Texture or (desc.Face > 0 and "rbxassetid://"..desc.Face or "rbxasset://textures/face.png")
        faceDecal.Parent = char.Head
        local newBC = Instance.new("BodyColors")
        newBC.HeadColor3 = desc.HeadColor
        newBC.TorsoColor3 = desc.TorsoColor
        newBC.LeftArmColor3 = desc.LeftArmColor
        newBC.RightArmColor3 = desc.RightArmColor
        newBC.LeftLegColor3 = desc.LeftLegColor
        newBC.RightLegColor3 = desc.RightLegColor
        newBC.Parent = char
        StartFixLoop(char, realColors, targetHeadTexture)
        for _, item in pairs(dummy:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("Accessory") then SmartWeld(char, item:Clone()) end
        end
        dummy:Destroy()
    end
    local function TransformarSkin(userId, username)
        if getgenv().IsApplyingSkin then return end
        getgenv().ApplyToken = (getgenv().ApplyToken or 0) + 1
        local myToken = getgenv().ApplyToken
        getgenv().IsApplyingSkin = true
        getgenv().CurrentSkinUserId = -1
        local char = Player.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not char or not hum then getgenv().IsApplyingSkin = false return end
        ClearAccessoryStates()
        local success, desc = pcall(function() return Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(userId) end)
        if not success or not desc then getgenv().IsApplyingSkin = false Notify("Falha ao aplicar skin") return end
        if myToken ~= getgenv().ApplyToken then getgenv().IsApplyingSkin = false return end
        getgenv().CurrentSkinUserId = userId
        ApplyDesc(desc)
        task.wait(0.2)
        if myToken ~= getgenv().ApplyToken then getgenv().IsApplyingSkin = false return end
        getgenv().IsApplyingSkin = false
        Notify("Applied: Skin " .. (username or userId))
    end
    ApplyBundleFunc = function(bundleId)
        task.spawn(function()
            ClearAccessoryStates()
            local ok, details = pcall(function() return AssetService:GetBundleDetailsAsync(bundleId) end)
            if not ok or not details then Notify("Falha ao carregar bundle") return end
            local outfitDesc = nil
            for _, item in ipairs(details.Items) do
                if item.Type == "UserOutfit" then
                    local ok2, d2 = pcall(function() return Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromOutfitId(item.Id) end)
                    if ok2 and d2 then outfitDesc = d2 break end
                end
            end
            if not outfitDesc then Notify("Bundle sem outfit") return end
            ApplyDesc(outfitDesc)
            Notify("Applied: Bundle " .. tostring(bundleId))
        end)
    end
    ApplyLegOnlyFunc = function(bundleId, legName)
        task.spawn(function()
            local char = Player.Character
            if not char then return end
            SaveOriginalLeg(char)
            RemoveCurrentLeg(char)
            local ok, details = pcall(function() return AssetService:GetBundleDetailsAsync(bundleId) end)
            if not ok or not details then Notify("Falha ao carregar perna") RestoreLeg(char) return end
            local outfitDesc = nil
            for _, item in ipairs(details.Items) do
                if item.Type == "UserOutfit" then
                    local ok2, d2 = pcall(function() return Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromOutfitId(item.Id) end)
                    if ok2 and d2 then outfitDesc = d2 break end
                end
            end
            if not outfitDesc then Notify("Bundle sem outfit") RestoreLeg(char) return end
            local dummy = Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(outfitDesc, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6)
            dummy.Name = "LegSource"
            dummy.Parent = workspace
            dummy:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0,-500,0))
            task.wait(0.4)
            local legMesh = nil
            for _, c in ipairs(dummy:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("CharacterMesh") and c.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.RightLeg then legMesh = c break end
            end
            if legMesh then legMesh:Clone().Parent = char CurrentLegBundle = bundleId Notify("Applied: " .. (legName or "Perna"))
            else Notify("Perna não encontrada") RestoreLeg(char) end
            dummy:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    ApplyAccessoryFunc = function(id)
        local char = Player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local obj = GetAsset(id)
        if not obj then Notify("Failed to load item ID: " .. tostring(id)) return end
        if obj:IsA("Accessory") or obj:IsA("Hat") then SmartWeld(char, obj) Notify("Accessory Equipped!")
        elseif obj:IsA("Tool") then obj.Parent = char Notify("Tool Equipped!")
        else obj.Parent = char Notify("Accessory Equipped!") end
    end
    ApplyHeadlessFunc = function(on)
        HeadlessOn = on
        local char = Player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then return end
        if on then
            SavedHeadTransparency = head.Transparency
            local mesh = head:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
            if mesh and not SavedHeadMesh then SavedHeadMesh = mesh:Clone() end
            local face = head:FindFirstChild("face") or head:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")
            if face and not SavedHeadFace then SavedHeadFace = face:Clone() end
            if mesh then mesh:Destroy() end
            if face then face:Destroy() end
            head.Transparency = 1
            head.CanCollide = false
            if HeadlessLoop then HeadlessLoop:Disconnect() end
            HeadlessLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not HeadlessOn then return end
                if not char or not char.Parent or not head or not head.Parent then
                    if HeadlessLoop then HeadlessLoop:Disconnect() HeadlessLoop = nil end
                    return
                end
                if head.Transparency ~= 1 then head.Transparency = 1 end
                local f = head:FindFirstChild("face") or head:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")
                if f then f:Destroy() end
                local m = head:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
                if m then m:Destroy() end
            end)
            Notify("Applied: Headless")
        else
            if HeadlessLoop then HeadlessLoop:Disconnect() HeadlessLoop = nil end
            head.Transparency = SavedHeadTransparency or 0
            head.CanCollide = true
            local mesh = head:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
            if mesh then mesh:Destroy() end
            if SavedHeadMesh then SavedHeadMesh:Clone().Parent = head SavedHeadMesh = nil end
            local face = head:FindFirstChild("face") or head:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")
            if face then face:Destroy() end
            if SavedHeadFace then SavedHeadFace:Clone().Parent = head SavedHeadFace = nil end
            Notify("Removido: Headless")
        end
    end
    ApplyRoyalBackFunc = function(on)
        RoyalOn = on
        local char = Player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local function RemoveRoyal()
            for _, v in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if (v:IsA("Accessory") or v:IsA("Hat") or v:IsA("Tool")) and (v.Name == "RoyalScepter" or v.Name == "Royal Scepter") then v:Destroy() end
            end
        end
        if not on then RemoveRoyal() Notify("Removido: Royal Scepter") return end
        RemoveRoyal()
        local obj = GetAsset(123021068422074)
        if not obj then Notify("Failed to load Royal") return end
        obj.Name = "RoyalScepter"
        if obj:IsA("Accessory") or obj:IsA("Hat") then WeldBack(char, obj)
        elseif obj:IsA("Tool") then
            local handle = obj:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if handle then
                obj.Parent = char
                handle.Anchored = false
                handle.CanCollide = false
                local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                local w = Instance.new("Weld")
                w.Part0 = torso
                w.Part1 = handle
                w.C0 = CFrame.new(0.25, -0.1, 0.6)
                w.C1 = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
                w.Parent = handle
            else obj.Parent = char end
        else obj.Parent = char end
        Notify("Applied: Royal Scepter nas costas")
    end
    local function ShowSkinPopup(userId, username)
        local oldBlur = Lighting:FindFirstChild("SkinPopupBlur")
        if oldBlur then oldBlur:Destroy() end
        if gui:FindFirstChild("SkinPopupOverlay") then gui:FindFirstChild("SkinPopupOverlay"):Destroy() end
        local blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
        blur.Name = "SkinPopupBlur"
        blur.Size = 12
        blur.Parent = Lighting
        local overlay = Instance.new("Frame", gui)
        overlay.Name = "SkinPopupOverlay"
        overlay.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        overlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        overlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        overlay.ZIndex = 1000
        overlay.Active = true
        local popupFrame = Instance.new("Frame", overlay)
        popupFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 170)
        popupFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        popupFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        popupFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(6, 6, 6)
        popupFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
        popupFrame.ZIndex = 1001
        Instance.new("UICorner", popupFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local popupStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", popupFrame)
        popupStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
        popupStroke.Thickness = 1
        local popupTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", popupFrame)
        popupTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
        popupTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        popupTitle.Text = "SKIN FOUND"
        popupTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        popupTitle.TextSize = 15
        popupTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        popupTitle.ZIndex = 1002
        local avatarImg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", popupFrame)
        avatarImg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 60)
        avatarImg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 25, 0, 50)
        avatarImg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        avatarImg.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId="..userId.."&width=150&height=150&format=png"
        avatarImg.ZIndex = 1002
        Instance.new("UICorner", avatarImg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", popupFrame)
        nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 20)
        nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 95, 0, 70)
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLabel.Text = username
        nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nameLabel.TextSize = 14
        nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
        nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLabel.ZIndex = 1002
        local applyBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", popupFrame)
        applyBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 32)
        applyBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 25, 1, -50)
        applyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
        applyBtn.Text = "Apply Skin"
        applyBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        applyBtn.TextSize = 13
        applyBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        applyBtn.ZIndex = 1003
        applyBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        Instance.new("UICorner", applyBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local cancelBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", popupFrame)
        cancelBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 32)
        cancelBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -145, 1, -50)
        cancelBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        cancelBtn.Text = "Cancel"
        cancelBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        cancelBtn.TextSize = 13
        cancelBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 59, 59)
        cancelBtn.ZIndex = 1003
        cancelBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        applyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            blur:Destroy()
            overlay:Destroy()
            Notify("Applying: " .. username)
            TransformarSkin(userId, username)
        end)
        cancelBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() blur:Destroy() overlay:Destroy() end)
        applyBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(applyBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play() end)
        applyBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(applyBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)}):Play() end)
        cancelBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(cancelBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play() end)
        cancelBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(cancelBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)}):Play() end)
    end
    local function AnimatePlayerButtons()
        local delayCount = 0
        for _, c in pairs(dropdownList:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("TextButton") then
                delayCount = delayCount + 1
                c.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                local lbl = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                if lbl then lbl.TextTransparency = 1 end
                local thumb = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("ImageLabel")
                if thumb then thumb.ImageTransparency = 1 end
                local idx = delayCount
                task.delay(idx * 0.03, function()
                    TweenService:Create(c, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.3}):Play()
                    if lbl then TweenService:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {TextTransparency = 0}):Play() end
                    if thumb then TweenService:Create(thumb, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {ImageTransparency = 0}):Play() end
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    local function OpenDropdown()
        if animating then return end
        animating = true
        dropdownOpen = true
        dropdownList.Visible = true
        local qtd = math.min(math.max(#Players:GetPlayers()-1, 1), 5)
        local targetHeight = qtd * 34 + 8
        TweenService:Create(arrow, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Rotation = 180}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(targetStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(150,150,150)}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(dropdownList, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, targetHeight),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 40)
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(dropStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
        AnimatePlayerButtons()
        task.delay(0.3, function() animating = false end)
    end
    local function CloseDropdown(instant)
        if animating and not instant then return end
        dropdownOpen = false
        if instant then
            dropdownList.Visible = false
            dropdownList.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            arrow.Rotation = 0
            return
        end
        animating = true
        TweenService:Create(arrow, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Rotation = 0}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(targetStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(50,50,50)}):Play()
        local tween = TweenService:Create(dropdownList, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1
        })
        TweenService:Create(dropStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.22), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
        tween:Play()
        tween.Completed:Connect(function()
            dropdownList.Visible = false
            animating = false
        end)
    end
    local function RefreshPlayerList()
        for _, c in pairs(dropdownList:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("TextButton") or c:IsA("TextLabel") then c:Destroy() end
        end
        local count = 0
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= Player then
                count = count + 1
                local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", dropdownList)
                btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20,20,20)
                btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                btn.Text = ""
                btn.AutoButtonColor = false
                btn.ZIndex = 61
                Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)
                local thumb = Instance.new("ImageLabel", btn)
                thumb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24)
                thumb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -12)
                thumb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                thumb.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId="..plr.UserId.."&width=100&height=100&format=png"
                thumb.ImageTransparency = 1
                thumb.ZIndex = 62
                Instance.new("UICorner", thumb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,4)
                local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
                lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
                lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 0)
                lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                lbl.Text = plr.Name .. (plr.DisplayName ~= plr.Name and " ("..plr.DisplayName..")" or "")
                lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                lbl.TextSize = 12
                lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220)
                lbl.TextTransparency = 1
                lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                lbl.ZIndex = 62
                btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.1}):Play() end)
                btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.3}):Play() end)
                btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    targetBtn.Text = "Target: "..plr.Name
                    CloseDropdown()
                    ShowSkinPopup(plr.UserId, plr.Name)
                end)
            end
        end
        if count == 0 then
            local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel", dropdownList)
            empty.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
            empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            empty.Text = "Nenhum player"
            empty.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            empty.TextSize = 12
            empty.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120,120,120)
            empty.ZIndex = 61
        end
    end
    targetBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if dropdownOpen then CloseDropdown() else RefreshPlayerList() OpenDropdown() end
    end)
    targetBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(targetStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(100,100,100)}):Play() end)
    targetBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not dropdownOpen then TweenService:Create(targetStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(50,50,50)}):Play() end
    end)
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
        if dropdownOpen then
            RefreshPlayerList()
            AnimatePlayerButtons()
            local qtd = math.min(math.max(#Players:GetPlayers()-1, 1), 5)
            TweenService:Create(dropdownList, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, qtd * 34 + 8)}):Play()
        end
    end)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function()
        if dropdownOpen then RefreshPlayerList() AnimatePlayerButtons() end
    end)
    local function searchUser()
        if searchBox.Text == "" then return end
        local success, userId = pcall(function() return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(searchBox.Text) end)
        if success then ShowSkinPopup(userId, searchBox.Text) else Notify("User not found") end
    end
    searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then searchUser() end end)
    searchIcon.MouseButton1Click:Connect(searchUser)
    local function ResetSkin()
        if getgenv().IsApplyingSkin then return end
        Notify("Applied: Reset Skin")
        TweenService:Create(resetBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.3}):Play()
        task.delay(0.1, function() TweenService:Create(resetBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65}):Play() end)
        ClearAccessoryStates()
        getgenv().CurrentSkinUserId = -1
        targetBtn.Text = "Target Player: Select..."
        searchBox.Text = ""
        TransformarSkin(Player.UserId, Player.Name)
    end
    resetBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(ResetSkin)
    resetBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(resetBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.4}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(resetStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(100,100,100)}):Play()
    end)
    resetBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(resetBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.65}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(resetStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(50,50,50)}):Play()
    end)
    local skinList = {
        {name = "KayquezinXD", id = 7445816356},
        {name = "Foxygaimar", id = 534577197},
        {name = "Meshew", id = 471532382},

        {name = "nyviima", id = 9264913030},

        {name = "Frecxy_Angels", id = 3923504748},

        {name = "Znerx3ys", id = 708884161},

        {name = "TryNotToRage", id = 10279120783},
        {name = "DraxynSoulx", id = 2396362636},
        {name = "Gaie_VR", id = 1244277839},
        {name = "totallyvelez", id = 3597977804},
        {name = "steik00s", id = 4644831551},
        {name = "1Dxqzz", id = 2425366002},
        {name = "Guime_blox", id = 2633091297},
        {name = "Mwaiconn", id = 3948962607},
    }
    for i, skin in ipairs(skinList) do
        local card = Instance.new("TextButton", skinGrid)
        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(6,6,6)
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        card.Text = ""

        card.LayoutOrder = i

        card.ZIndex = 2
        Instance.new("UICorner", card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)
        local thumb = Instance.new("ImageLabel", card)
        thumb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 32)
        thumb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -16)
        thumb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        thumb.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId="..skin.id.."&width=150&height=150&format=png"
        thumb.ZIndex = 1
        Instance.new("UICorner", thumb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,4)
        local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 1, 0)
        nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 0)
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLabel.Text = skin.name
        nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nameLabel.TextSize = 11
        nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220)
        nameLabel.TextWrapped = true
        nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
        nameLabel.ZIndex = 1
        card.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

            TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.05), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.1}):Play()
            task.wait(0.05)
            TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.05), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.3}):Play()
            ShowSkinPopup(skin.id, skin.name)
        end)
        card.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.15}):Play() end)
        card.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.3}):Play() end)
    end
end

do
    local settingsPageH = pages["Setting"]
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local Player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = Player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    pcall(function() setfpscap(0) end)

    for _,v in pairs(settingsPageH:GetChildren()) do
        if v:IsA("Frame") then v:Destroy() end
    end

    local function GetMainFrame()
        if frame then return frame end
        local ui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("EclipseUI")
        if not ui then
            ui = playerGui:WaitForChild("EclipseUI", 5)
        end
        if not ui then return nil end
        return ui:FindFirstChild("Main", true)
    end

    if not isfolder("FTF_Eclipse") then makefolder("FTF_Eclipse") end

    local backgrounds = {

    }

    getgenv().BackgroundLocked = getgenv().BackgroundLocked or false
    getgenv().ShowFPS = getgenv().ShowFPS or false
    getgenv().ShowPing = getgenv().ShowPing or false
    getgenv().ShowCPU = getgenv().ShowCPU or false
    if getgenv().TouchScreen == nil then getgenv().TouchScreen = true end

    local function SaveConfig()
        local data = {
            BackgroundID = getgenv().CustomBackgroundID or "",
            UIBackground = getgenv().UIBackground or "Style 1",
            Font = getgenv().UIFont or "Gotham",
            BackgroundLocked = getgenv().BackgroundLocked,
            ShowFPS = getgenv().ShowFPS, ShowPing = getgenv().ShowPing, ShowCPU = getgenv().ShowCPU,
            TouchScreen = getgenv().TouchScreen,
            ShineColor = getgenv().ShineColor and {R = getgenv().ShineColor.R, G = getgenv().ShineColor.G, B = getgenv().ShineColor.B} or nil,
        }
        pcall(function()
            writefile("FTF_Eclipse/config.json", HttpService:JSONEncode(data))
        end)
    end

    local function LoadConfig()
        if isfile("FTF_Eclipse/config.json") then
            local success, data = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile("FTF_Eclipse/config.json"))
            end)
            if success and data then
                getgenv().CustomBackgroundID = data.BackgroundID or ""
                getgenv().UIBackground = data.UIBackground or "Style 1"
                getgenv().UIFont = data.Font or "Gotham"
                getgenv().BackgroundLocked = data.BackgroundLocked or false
                getgenv().ShowFPS = data.ShowFPS or false
                getgenv().ShowPing = data.ShowPing or false
                getgenv().ShowCPU = data.ShowCPU or false
                if data.TouchScreen == nil then
                    getgenv().TouchScreen = true
                else
                    getgenv().TouchScreen = data.TouchScreen
                end
                if data.ShineColor then
                    getgenv().ShineColor = Color3.new(data.ShineColor.R, data.ShineColor.G, data.ShineColor.B)
                end
                return data
            end
        end
        getgenv().UIBackground = "Style 1"
        getgenv().UIFont = "Gotham"
        getgenv().CustomBackgroundID = backgrounds[1][2]
        getgenv().BackgroundLocked = false
        if getgenv().TouchScreen == nil then getgenv().TouchScreen = true end
        SaveConfig()
        return nil
    end

    local function UpdateBackground(id, force)
        if getgenv().BackgroundLocked and not force then return end
        local mainFrame = GetMainFrame()
        if not mainFrame then return end
        local oldBg = mainFrame:FindFirstChild("CustomBg")
        if oldBg then oldBg:Destroy() end
        if id and id ~= "" then
            local bg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", mainFrame)
            bg.Name = "CustomBg"
            bg.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
            bg.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
            bg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            bg.Image = "rbxassetid://".. tostring(id)
            bg.ImageTransparency = 0.15
            bg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
            bg.ZIndex = 0
        end
    end

    LoadConfig()

    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.5)
        pcall(function()
            if CrosshairLayer then CrosshairLayer.Visible = getgenv().TouchScreen end
        end)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        for i = 1, 20 do
            local mainFrame = GetMainFrame()
            if mainFrame then break end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
        local id = getgenv().CustomBackgroundID
        if not id or id == "" then
            for _, b in ipairs(backgrounds) do
                if b[1] == getgenv().UIBackground then
                    id = b[2]
                    break
                end
            end
            if not id or id == "" then id = backgrounds[1][2] end
            getgenv().CustomBackgroundID = id
        end
        UpdateBackground(id, true)
    end)

    local container = Instance.new("Frame", settingsPageH)
    container.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
    container.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    container.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Instance.new("UIPadding", container).PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10)
    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", container)
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    local CurrentFont = Enum.Font[getgenv().UIFont] or Enum.Font.Gotham
    local function ApplyFont(font)
        CurrentFont = font
        local mainFrame = GetMainFrame()
        if mainFrame then
            for _, obj in ipairs(mainFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
                    obj.Font = font
                end
            end
        end
        if fpsLabel then fpsLabel.Font = font end
    end
    ApplyFont(CurrentFont)

    local function CreateGroupbox(parent, title)
        local box = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        box.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
        box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        box.BorderSizePixel = 0
        box.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,8)
        Instance.new("UIStroke", box).Color = Color3.fromRGB(70,70,70)

        local titleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", box)
        titleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,-20,0,20)
        titleLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0,0)
        titleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        titleLabel.Text = title
        titleLabel.Font = CurrentFont
        titleLabel.TextSize = 14
        titleLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235,235,235)
        titleLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

        local content = Instance.new("Frame", box)
        content.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
        content.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,22)
        content.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        content.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y

        local cl = Instance.new("UIListLayout", content)
        cl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
        cl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

        return box, content
    end

    local boxSettings, contentSettings = CreateGroupbox(container, "Appearance")

    local function CreateDropdown(parent, labelText, list, default, callback)
        local holder = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,35)
        holder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        holder.ClipsDescendants = false
        holder.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        Instance.new("UICorner", holder).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)
        Instance.new("UIListLayout", holder).Padding = UDim.new(0,0)

        local top = Instance.new("Frame", holder)
        top.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,35)
        top.BackgroundTransparency = 1

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", top)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1,-40,1,0)
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0,15,0,0)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = labelText..": "..default
        label.Font = CurrentFont
        label.TextSize = 14
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235,235,235)
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

        local arrow = Instance.new("TextLabel", top)
        arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0,20,1,0)
        arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1,-25,0,0)
        arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        arrow.Text = "v"
        arrow.Font = CurrentFont
        arrow.TextSize = 12
        arrow.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180,180,180)

        local clickArea = Instance.new("TextButton", top)
        clickArea.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
        clickArea.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        clickArea.Text = ""

        local dropdownFrame = Instance.new("Frame", holder)
        dropdownFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1,-8,0,0)
        dropdownFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0,4,0,0)
        dropdownFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        dropdownFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
        dropdownFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
        Instance.new("UICorner", dropdownFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)
        Instance.new("UIStroke", dropdownFrame).Color = Color3.fromRGB(45,45,50)
        Instance.new("UIListLayout", dropdownFrame).Padding = UDim.new(0,0)

        local isOpen = false
        local dropdownHeight = #list * 34

        for i, data in ipairs(list) do
            local valueName = type(data) == "table" and data[1] or data
            local option = Instance.new("TextButton", dropdownFrame)
            option.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,34)
            option.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            option.Text = valueName
            option.Font = CurrentFont
            option.TextSize = 14
            option.TextColor3 = option.Text == default and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255) or Color3.fromRGB(170,170,170)
            option.BorderSizePixel = 0
            option.AutoButtonColor = false

            if i < #list then
                local line = Instance.new("Frame", option)
                line.Size = UDim2.new(1,-20,0,1)
                line.Position = UDim2.new(0,10,1,0)
                line.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35,35,40)
                line.BorderSizePixel = 0
                line.ZIndex = 2
            end

            option.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local value = type(data) == "table" and data[1] or data
                local id = type(data) == "table" and data[2] or ""
                label.Text = labelText..": "..value
                for _, opt in pairs(dropdownFrame:GetChildren()) do
                    if opt:IsA("TextButton") then opt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170,170,170) end
                end
                option.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)

                getgenv().UIBackground = value
                getgenv().CustomBackgroundID = id
                getgenv().BackgroundLocked = true
                UpdateBackground(id, true)
                SaveConfig()

                TweenService:Create(dropdownFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {Size = UDim2.new(1,-8,0,0)}):Play()
                arrow.Text = "v"
                task.wait(0.25)
                isOpen = false
            end)
        end

        clickArea.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            isOpen = not isOpen
            if isOpen then
                arrow.Text = "^"
                TweenService:Create(dropdownFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {Size = UDim2.new(1,-8,0,dropdownHeight)}):Play()
            else
                arrow.Text = "v"
                TweenService:Create(dropdownFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {Size = UDim2.new(1,-8,0,0)}):Play()
            end
        end)
    end

    CreateDropdown(contentSettings, "UI Background", backgrounds, getgenv().UIBackground, function(data) end)

    do
        local holder = Instance.new("Frame", contentSettings)
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,35)
        holder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        Instance.new("UICorner", holder).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)
        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", holder)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0,15,0,0)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = "Custom Background ID"
        label.Font = CurrentFont
        label.TextSize = 14
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        local inputBox = Instance.new("TextBox", holder)
        inputBox.Size = UDim2.new(0,80,0,24)
        inputBox.Position = UDim2.new(1,-90,0.5,-12)
        inputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
        inputBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
        inputBox.PlaceholderText = "ID..."
        inputBox.Font = CurrentFont
        inputBox.TextSize = 13
        inputBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235,235,235)
        inputBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100,100,100)
        inputBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        inputBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", inputBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,5)
        inputBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
            if enter then
                local id = inputBox.Text:match("%d+")
                if id then
                    getgenv().CustomBackgroundID = id
                    getgenv().UIBackground = "Custom"
                    getgenv().BackgroundLocked = true
                    UpdateBackground(id, true)
                    SaveConfig()
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local fonts = {}
    for _, font in pairs(Enum.Font:GetEnumItems()) do table.insert(fonts, font.Name) end
    table.sort(fonts)
    CreateDropdown(contentSettings, "UI Font", fonts, getgenv().UIFont, function(fontName)
        getgenv().UIFont = fontName
        ApplyFont(Enum.Font[fontName])
        SaveConfig()
    end)

do
    local holder = Instance.new("Frame", contentSettings)
    holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 68)
    holder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    Instance.new("UICorner", holder).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", holder)
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 6)

        Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
        Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 120),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0),
        Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 255),

        Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 0),
    }

    local function ApplyShineColor(color)
        getgenv().ShineColor = color
        preview.BackgroundColor3 = color
        pcall(SaveConfig)
        for _, data in pairs(tabShines) do
            if data.gradient and data.gradient.Parent then
                data.gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, color),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
                })
                data.gradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
                })
            end
        end
    end

    if getgenv().ShineColor then
        task.delay(0.5, function() ApplyShineColor(getgenv().ShineColor) end)
    end

    local x = 15
    for _, col in ipairs(colors) do
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", holder)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 34)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = col
        btn.Text = ""
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        Instance.new("UIStroke", btn).Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            ApplyShineColor(col)
            Notify("Theme System", "Shine color applied!", 3)
            TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)}):Play()
            task.delay(0.1, function()
                TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)}):Play()
            end)
        end)
        x += 28
    end

    local hexBox = Instance.new("TextBox", holder)
    hexBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 22)
    hexBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 34)
    hexBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
    hexBox.PlaceholderText = "HEX"
    hexBox.Text = ""
    hexBox.Font = CurrentFont
    hexBox.TextSize = 12
    hexBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    hexBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100)
    hexBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    hexBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Instance.new("UICorner", hexBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    hexBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
        if not enter then return end
        local hex = hexBox.Text:gsub("#", ""):gsub("%s+", "")
        if #hex == 6 then
            local ok, col = pcall(function() return Color3.fromHex(hex) end)
            if ok and col then
                ApplyShineColor(col)
                Notify("FTF Eclipse V3.2", "Shine color applied!", 3)
            end
        end
    end)
end

    local function CreateToggle(parent, text, default, callback)
        local toggleFrame = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        toggleFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
        toggleFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", toggleFrame)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, 0)
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 0)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = text
        label.Font = CurrentFont
        label.TextSize = 13
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

        local toggle = Instance.new("TextButton", toggleFrame)
        toggle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 17)
        toggle.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0.5, -9)
        toggle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        toggle.BackgroundTransparency = default and 0 or 1
        toggle.Text = ""
        toggle.AutoButtonColor = false
        Instance.new("UICorner", toggle).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", toggle)
        stroke.Color = default and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100)
        stroke.Thickness = 1.5

        local circle = Instance.new("Frame", toggle)
        circle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
        circle.Position = default and UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, -7) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -7)
        circle.BackgroundColor3 = default and Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30) or Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
        circle.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", circle).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local enabled = default
        toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            enabled = not enabled
            TweenService:Create(circle, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                Position = enabled and UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, -7) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -7),
                BackgroundColor3 = enabled and Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30) or Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(toggle, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                BackgroundTransparency = enabled and 0 or 1,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(stroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                Color = enabled and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100)
            }):Play()
            if callback then callback(enabled) end
            SaveConfig()
        end)
        return toggleFrame
    end

    local fpsGui, fpsLabel
    local fpsConnection

    local function CreateFPSCounter()
        if fpsGui then fpsGui:Destroy() end
        fpsGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        fpsGui.Name = "FPSCounter"
        fpsGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        fpsGui.Parent = playerGui

        local frame = Instance.new("Frame", fpsGui)
        frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 180, 0, 25)
        frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 1)
        frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

        fpsLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
        fpsLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        fpsLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        fpsLabel.Text = "FPS: 0 (Optimized)"
        fpsLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
        fpsLabel.Font = CurrentFont
        fpsLabel.TextSize = 14
        fpsLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center

        local lastTick = tick()
        local frameCount = 0

        fpsConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            frameCount = frameCount + 1
            if tick() - lastTick >= 0.25 then
                local currentFps = math.floor(frameCount / (tick() - lastTick) + 0.5)
                frameCount = 0
                lastTick = tick()

                local status = "(Optimized)"
                local color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)

                if currentFps < 30 then
                    status = "(Laggy)"
                    color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                elseif currentFps < 60 then
                    status = "(Normal)"
                    color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
                elseif currentFps >= 120 then
                    status = "(Ultra)"
                    color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)
                end

                fpsLabel.Text = "FPS: ".. currentFps.." ".. status
                fpsLabel.TextColor3 = color
            end
        end)
    end

    CreateToggle(contentStats, "Show FPS", getgenv().ShowFPS, function(state)
        getgenv().ShowFPS = state
        if state then
            CreateFPSCounter()
        else
            if fpsConnection then fpsConnection:Disconnect() end
            if fpsGui then fpsGui:Destroy() end
        end
    end)

    local pingLabel, cpuLabel
    CreateToggle(contentStats, "Show Ping", getgenv().ShowPing, function(state)
        getgenv().ShowPing = state
        if state and not pingLabel then
            local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui", playerGui)
            screenGui.Name = "PINGCounter"
            local frame = Instance.new("Frame", screenGui)
            frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 25) frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 30)
            frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
            pingLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
            pingLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) pingLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            pingLabel.Font = CurrentFont pingLabel.TextSize = 14 pingLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            task.spawn(function()
                while getgenv().ShowPing do
                    local ping = 0
                    pcall(function() ping = math.floor(Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()) end)
                    pingLabel.Text = "PING: ".. ping.."ms"
                    pingLabel.TextColor3 = ping > 150 and Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0) or ping > 80 and Color3.fromRGB(255,165,0) or Color3.fromRGB(0,255,0)
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
            end)
        elseif not state and pingLabel then pingLabel.Parent:Destroy() pingLabel = nil end
    end)

    CreateToggle(contentStats, "Show CPU", getgenv().ShowCPU, function(state)
        getgenv().ShowCPU = state
        if state and not cpuLabel then
            local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui", playerGui)
            screenGui.Name = "CPUCounter"
            local frame = Instance.new("Frame", screenGui)
            frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 25) frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 60)
            frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
            cpuLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
            cpuLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) cpuLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            cpuLabel.Font = CurrentFont cpuLabel.TextSize = 14 cpuLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            task.spawn(function()
                while getgenv().ShowCPU do
                    local physics = Stats.PerformanceStats:GetValue("PhysicsStepMs")
                    local render = Stats.PerformanceStats:GetValue("RenderMs")
                    local total = physics + render
                    cpuLabel.Text = string.format("CPU: %.1fms", total)
                    cpuLabel.TextColor3 = total > 16 and Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0) or total > 8 and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,0) or Color3.fromRGB(0,200,255)
                    task.wait(0.25)
                end
            end)
        elseif not state and cpuLabel then cpuLabel.Parent:Destroy() cpuLabel = nil end
    end)

local SelectedConfig = nil
local ConfigList = {}

local function SanitizeName(name)
    if not name then return "" end
    name = tostring(name):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    name = name:gsub("[^%w%-%_ ]", "")
    name = name:gsub("%s+", " ")
    name = name:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    return name
end

local function SafeEncode(v, seen)
    local t = typeof(v)
    if t == "boolean" or t == "string" then
        return v
    elseif t == "number" then
        if v ~= v or v == math.huge or v == -math.huge then return 0 end
        return v
    elseif t == "Color3" then
        return { __type = "Color3", R = v.R, G = v.G, B = v.B }
    elseif t == "table" then
        seen = seen or {}
        if seen[v] then return nil end
        seen[v] = true
        local out = {}
        for k2, v2 in pairs(v) do
            if type(k2) == "string" or type(k2) == "number" then
                local enc = SafeEncode(v2, seen)
                if enc ~= nil then out[k2] = enc end
            end
        end
        seen[v] = nil
        return out
    else
        return nil
    end
end

local function SafeJSONEncode(data)
    local ok, json = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONEncode(data) end)
    if not ok or not json then return nil end
    local ok2 = pcall(function() HttpService:JSONDecode(json) end)
    if not ok2 then return nil end
    return json
end

local function GatherAll()
    local data = {}
    for k, v in pairs(getgenv()) do
        local t = typeof(v)
        if t == "boolean" or t == "number" or t == "string" or t == "Color3" then
            if k ~= "BackgroundLocked" then
                local enc = SafeEncode(v)
                if enc ~= nil then data[k] = enc end
            end
        end
    end
    if getgenv().FTF_Sounds then data.FTF_Sounds = SafeEncode(getgenv().FTF_Sounds) end
    if getgenv().FTF_Shaders then data.FTF_Shaders = SafeEncode(getgenv().FTF_Shaders) end
    if getgenv().FTF_Calibration then data.FTF_Calibration = SafeEncode(getgenv().FTF_Calibration) end
    if getgenv().FTF_Textures then data.FTF_Textures = SafeEncode(getgenv().FTF_Textures) end
    if getgenv().FTF_JumpSkinID then data.FTF_JumpSkinID = getgenv().FTF_JumpSkinID end
    if getgenv().Eclipse_JumpSave then
        data.Eclipse_JumpData = SafeEncode(getgenv().Eclipse_JumpSave)
    elseif getgenv().Eclipse_JumpData then
        data.Eclipse_JumpData = SafeEncode(getgenv().Eclipse_JumpData)
    end
    data.TouchScreen = getgenv().TouchScreen
    data.ShowFPS = getgenv().ShowFPS
    data.ShowPing = getgenv().ShowPing
    data.ShowCPU = getgenv().ShowCPU
    data.UIBackground = getgenv().UIBackground
    data.CustomBackgroundID = getgenv().CustomBackgroundID
    data.UIFont = getgenv().UIFont
    return data
end

local function DecodeValue(v)
    if type(v) == "table" and v.__type == "Color3" then
        return Color3.new(v.R, v.G, v.B)
    elseif type(v) == "table" then
        local t = {}
        for k2, v2 in pairs(v) do
            t[k2] = DecodeValue(v2)
        end
        return t
    end
    return v
end

local function WriteConfig(name, data)
    local json = SafeJSONEncode(data)
    if not json then return false, "encode" end
    local path = "FTF_Eclipse/configs/"..name..".json"
    local ok = pcall(function() writefile(path, json) end)
    if not ok then return false, "writefile" end
    local ok2 = pcall(function()
        if not isfile or not isfile(path) then error("no file") end
        local back = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path))
        if type(back) ~= "table" then error("bad json") end
    end)
    if not ok2 then return false, "verify" end
    return true
end

local function ApplyAllLoaded(data)
    if not data then return end
    for k, v in pairs(data) do
        if k ~= "Eclipse_JumpData" then
            getgenv()[k] = DecodeValue(v)
        end
    end
    if data.Eclipse_JumpData and typeof(data.Eclipse_JumpData) == "table" then
        getgenv().Eclipse_JumpData = data.Eclipse_JumpData
        getgenv().Eclipse_JumpSave = data.Eclipse_JumpData
        pcall(function()
            if writefile then writefile("Eclipse_JumpButtons_Save.json", HttpService:JSONEncode(data.Eclipse_JumpData)) end
        end)
        if getgenv().Eclipse_ApplyJumpButtons then
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(0.5)
                pcall(getgenv().Eclipse_ApplyJumpButtons)
            end)
        end
    end
    if getgenv().Eclipse_ToggleRegistry then
        for key, setState in pairs(getgenv().Eclipse_ToggleRegistry) do
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(0.3)
                if getgenv()[key] ~= nil then
                    pcall(setState, getgenv()[key], true)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    if getgenv().Eclipse_ApplyFog then task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.4) pcall(getgenv().Eclipse_ApplyFog) end) end
    if getgenv().Eclipse_ApplySounds then task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.4) pcall(getgenv().Eclipse_ApplySounds) end) end
    if getgenv().Eclipse_ApplyShaders then task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.4) pcall(getgenv().Eclipse_ApplyShaders) end) end
    if getgenv().Eclipse_ApplyCalibration then task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.4) pcall(getgenv().Eclipse_ApplyCalibration) end) end
    if getgenv().Eclipse_ApplyTextures then task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.5) pcall(getgenv().Eclipse_ApplyTextures) end) end
    pcall(function() if CrosshairLayer then CrosshairLayer.Visible = getgenv().TouchScreen end end)
    pcall(function() if getgenv().CustomBackgroundID then UpdateBackground(getgenv().CustomBackgroundID, true) end end)
    pcall(function() if getgenv().UIFont then ApplyFont(Enum.Font[getgenv().UIFont] or Enum.Font.Gotham) end end)
    if getgenv().OnConfigLoaded then pcall(getgenv().OnConfigLoaded, data) end
    if SaveConfig then pcall(SaveConfig) end
end

local function GetConfigFiles()
    local list = {}
    pcall(function()
        if not listfiles then return end
        for _, path in ipairs(listfiles("FTF_Eclipse/configs")) do
            local name = tostring(path):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
            if name then table.insert(list, name) end
        end
    end)
    table.sort(list)
    return list
end

dropdownFrame.LayoutOrder = 2
dropdownFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 0)
dropdownFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5, 5, 5)
dropdownFrame.ClipsDescendants = true

local isOpen = false
local function RefreshDropdown()
    for _, c in ipairs(dropdownFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if c:IsA("TextButton") or c:IsA("TextLabel") then c:Destroy() end
    end
    ConfigList = GetConfigFiles()
    local h = 0
    if #ConfigList == 0 then
        local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel", dropdownFrame)
        empty.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34)
        empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        empty.Text = "Nenhuma config salva"
        empty.Font = CurrentFont
        empty.TextSize = 13
        empty.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120)
        empty.ZIndex = 10
        h = 34
    else
        for _, name in ipairs(ConfigList) do
            local opt = Instance.new("TextButton", dropdownFrame)
            opt.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34)
            opt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            opt.Text = name
            opt.Font = CurrentFont
            opt.TextSize = 14
            opt.TextColor3 = (name == SelectedConfig) and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170)
            opt.AutoButtonColor = false
            opt.ZIndex = 10
            opt.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                SelectedConfig = name
                dropLabel.Text = "Config: "..name
                isOpen = false
                dropArrow.Text = "v"
                TweenService:Create(dropdownFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 0)}):Play()
            end)
            h += 34
        end
    end
    if isOpen then
        TweenService:Create(dropdownFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, h)}):Play()
    end
end

local dropClick = Instance.new("TextButton", dropTop)
dropClick.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
dropClick.BackgroundTransparency = 1
dropClick.Text = ""
dropClick.ZIndex = 12
dropClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    isOpen = not isOpen
    RefreshDropdown()
    if isOpen then
        dropArrow.Text = "^"
        local h = #ConfigList == 0 and 34 or (#ConfigList * 34)
        TweenService:Create(dropdownFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, h)}):Play()
    else
        dropArrow.Text = "v"
        TweenService:Create(dropdownFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 0)}):Play()
    end
end)
RefreshDropdown()
